# Archicad API coverage

Every function of the Archicad API (C functions, the C++ API and the Archicad 25/26 command ids), and whether the Tapir Add-On uses it. Generated by `tools/generate_api_coverage.py`; do not edit by hand.

**2159 of 2181** applicable Archicad API items are used by Tapir (98 %): ✅ 1859 done, 🔀 300 merged, 🚧 19 in progress, ⬜ 3 open, ⛔ 28 skipped, ➖ 670 not applicable.

| Part of the API | Used | Applicable | ✅ | 🔀 | 🚧 | 🙋 | ⬜ | ⛔ | ➖ |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C functions | 98 % | 695 | 599 | 88 | 7 |  | 1 | 10 | 208 |
| C functions of Archicad 25/26 only, and their command ids | 95 % | 63 | 53 | 7 | 1 |  | 2 | 6 | 70 |
| C++ API (ACAPI::, IFCAPI::) | 99 % | 1423 | 1207 | 205 | 11 |  |  | 12 | 392 |

Archicad 25–30 DevKits; updated 2026-11-04 15:46 UTC.

| | Status |
|---|---|
| ✅ | used on `main` |
| 🔀 | used on `main`, not yet in a release |
| 🚧 | an open pull request adds it |
| 🙋 | an open issue names it: someone is on it, check there before starting |
| ⬜ | open: file an "Archicad API request" issue naming it to claim it |
| ⛔ | a maintainer decided not to expose it (reason given) |
| ➖ | not usable from a JSON command (reason given) |

Names are the newest ones; older names (renamed functions, AC25/26 command ids) are listed with them. "Used" means Tapir calls it somewhere, not that every option is exposed. C++ member functions count as used when a file that uses their class calls a function of that name.

## Element

- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_AddClassificationItem` – Classifies an element. — [#1292](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1292 "Phase 0 of optimizations. In-process Criteria Evaluator and Search Commands (#1253)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_AddClassificationItemDefault` – Classifies an element.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_AttachObserver` – Attaches an observer to the given element. — [#1842](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1842 "SetElementNotificationClient: optional element types to observe, touch only changed observers"), [#1840](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1840 "SetElementNotificationClient with modification notifications blocks Archicad for a long time on large projects")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_CalcBounds` – Returns the model extent of an element in the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_CalcBoundsID`)</sub> — [#1835](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1835 "Get3DBoundingBoxes: measure Walls on their 3D model, with their connections")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_CalcCoreOffsetToSideOffset` – Converts the reveal depth offset from core skin to wall side for wall openings. <sub>(was `APIAny_CalcCoreOffsetToSideOffsetID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_CalcSideOffsetToCoreOffset` – Converts the reveal depth offset from wall side to core skin for wall openings. <sub>(was `APIAny_CalcSideOffsetToCoreOffsetID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_Element_CatchChangeDefaults` – Register or unregister your add-on to be notified if the default settings of a given element type is modified. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchChangeDefaults`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Element_CatchNewElement` – Register or unregister your add-on to be notified if the given type of element is created. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchNewElement`)</sub> — [#1825](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1825 "Element notifications: changes and deletions made by Tapir commands are not reported") *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Change` — [#1802](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1802 "Experimental ModifyCurtainWallFrames: contour, class and type of single frames (#811)"), [#1748](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1748 "Roofs: read and set the planes of each pivot polygon edge (#569)"), [#1671](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1671 "Drawing anchor placement, offset, and pen table control"), [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#463](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/463 "SetDetailsOfElements: modify any element type with the Modify commands' type-specific parameters"), [#371](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/371 "Lessons learned building a Tapir-based automation toolkit (AC29)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeContent` – Change the content of a text or a textual label element. <sub>(was `APIAny_ChangeContentID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeDefaults` – Changes the default parameters of an element. — [#1826](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1826 "CreateMorphs: favoriteName, layerIndex and id per item")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeDefaultsExt` – Changes the default parameters of the Window, Door, Section, Elevation, Interior Elevation, ChangeMarker, Detail, Worksheet, or Stair default element and its marker(s). This function also allows you to change the default settings of the Curtain Wall and its subelem.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeElementInfoString` – Changes the compound info string of the given element. <sub>(was `APIDb_ChangeElementInfoStringID`)</sub> — [#371](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/371 "Lessons learned building a Tapir-based automation toolkit (AC29)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeExt` – Modifies a Window, Door, Section, Elevation, Interior Elevation, ChangeMarker, Detail, Worksheet, Stair or External element in the current database. — [#1377](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1377 "Place Detail and Worksheet markers, and change their settings"), [#811](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/811 "Get/Set door & window marker settings, and Curtain Wall frame/panel class assignment")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeMemo` – Changes the memo part of certain element types.
- ⛔ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeMore` – Modifies a number of elements in the current database. *(no speed gain over SetDetailsOfElements / the Modify* commands in the #1226 measurement, and one error code for the whole batch instead of per-element results)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeMorphEdgeType` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Modifies a morph element's edge type in the current database.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ChangeParameters` – Changes the parameters of a number of elements. — [#1671](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1671 "Drawing anchor placement, offset, and pen table control"), [#1292](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1292 "Phase 0 of optimizations. In-process Criteria Evaluator and Search Commands (#1253)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Create` — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_CreateExt` – Places a new Window, Door, Skylight, Section, Elevation, Interior Elevation, ChangeMarker, Detail, or Worksheet element with the specified marker into the current database. — [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#1377](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1377 "Place Detail and Worksheet markers, and change their settings")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Element_Decompose` – Enumerates the planes of a Multi-plane Roof.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Delete` – Deletes a number of elements. — [#1826](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1826 "CreateMorphs: favoriteName, layerIndex and id per item")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_DeleteSelected` – Deletes the selected elements.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_DetachObserver` – Detaches the observer from the given element. — [#1842](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1842 "SetElementNotificationClient: optional element types to observe, touch only changed observers"), [#1840](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1840 "SetElementNotificationClient with modification notifications blocks Archicad for a long time on large projects")
- ➖ `ACAPI_Element_DrawElement` – Shows/draws an existing element. <sub>(was `APIDb_DrawElementID`)</sub> *(draws into the add-on's drawing-data database or own window, only useful inside one add-on call)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Edit` – Transforms a set of elements.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Elem2UIPriority` – Returns user interface priority for a building material connection priority. <sub>(was `APIAny_Elem2UIPriorityID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_ElemHasVisibleSkin` – Returns if elem has visible skin depending on Partial Structure Display. <sub>(was `APIAny_ElemHasVisibleSkinID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_Element_ElemTypeToNeigID` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns a neig for the given element type. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Goodies_ElemTypeToNeigID`)</sub> *(neig ids, an internal of interactive input and selection)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Filter` – Tests an element by some given criteria.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_Get` – Returns information on the main parameters of a given element. — [#1841](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1841 "Check sub-element objects without reading every object in full"), [#1802](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1802 "Experimental ModifyCurtainWallFrames: contour, class and type of single frames (#811)"), [#1671](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1671 "Drawing anchor placement, offset, and pen table control"), [#1506](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1506 "[4/4] Add GetLibraryPartFileInfo: read a .gsm file without loading it"), [#1292](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1292 "Phase 0 of optimizations. In-process Criteria Evaluator and Search Commands (#1253)"), [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#463](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/463 "SetDetailsOfElements: modify any element type with the Modify commands' type-specific parameters"), [#1839](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1839 "GetAllElements and GetElementsByType Object take minutes on large projects (sub-element object check)"), [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetActualOverrideStyle` – Get actual (merged) visual override style for an element.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetClassificationInSystem` – Retrieves a classification of an elem in a given classification system.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetClassificationInSystemDefault` – Retrieves a classification of a default elem in a given classification system.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetClassificationItems` – Retrieves all of the classifications of an element. — [#1537](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1537 "[5/5] Favorites through the Favorite manager, and library part parameter values")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetClassificationItemsDefault` – Retrieves all of the classifications of a default elem.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetCollisions` – Returns the collisions between two groups.
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Element_GetComponents` – Retrieves the components of an element.
- ⛔ `ACAPI_Element_GetComponents_Obsolete` – Returns the components of the element. *(obsolete; replaced by ACAPI_Element_GetComponents (#1019))*
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Element_GetCompoundInfoString` – Returns the compound info string of the given element. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetCompoundInfoStringID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetDefaults` – Retrieves the actual default settings for the element of type. — [#1826](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1826 "CreateMorphs: favoriteName, layerIndex and id per item"), [#1702](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1702 "Add CreateShells: extruded, revolved and ruled shells")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetDefaultsExt` – Retrieves the actual default settings for the Window, Door, Section, Elevation, Interior Elevation, ChangeMarker, Detail, Worksheet, or Stair element with its marker(s). You are also able to obtain the Curtain Wall and its sub-element default settings via this function. — [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#1377](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1377 "Place Detail and Worksheet markers, and change their settings")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetDescriptors` – Returns the descriptors of an element.
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Element_GetElemCreatorToolUnId` – Returns the unique ID of the creator tool of the specified object. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetElemCreatorToolUnIdID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetElemList` – Returns an array of guids of the elements of the given type. — [#1842](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1842 "SetElementNotificationClient: optional element types to observe, touch only changed observers"), [#1841](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1841 "Check sub-element objects without reading every object in full"), [#1834](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1834 "[3/3] Notification clients: attribute replacement events"), [#1831](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1831 "[2/3] Notification clients: window, floor and project events"), [#1823](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1823 "[1/3] Notification clients: selection changes"), [#1292](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1292 "Phase 0 of optimizations. In-process Criteria Evaluator and Search Commands (#1253)"), [#1840](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1840 "SetElementNotificationClient with modification notifications blocks Archicad for a long time on large projects"), [#1813](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1813 "Notification clients: selection, window and project events")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetElemTypeName` – Returns the localized name of the built-in element types. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Goodies_GetElemTypeName`, `APIAny_GetElemTypeNameID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetElementFromAnywhere` – Returns the element referred by guid irrespectively of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetElementFromAnywhereID`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Element_GetElementInfoString` – Returns the compound info string of the given element. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetElementInfoStringID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetElementURLRef` – Retrieves the URL reference assigned to an element. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetElementURLRefID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetElementsWithClassification` – Query elements with the given classification.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Element_GetExtent` – Returns the drawing extent of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetExtentID`)</sub>

## Analytical

- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_AddLoadCaseToLoadCombination` – Creates an Analytical Load Combination. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_AddLoadCaseToLoadCombination`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_ConvertAnalyticalLinkToNonShortest` – Returns the Structural Analytical Link %Geometry. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_ConvertAnalyticalLinkToNonShortest`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_CreateAnalyticalLoadCase` – Returns the Load Case with the given unique identifier. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_CreateAnalyticalLoadCase`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_CreateAnalyticalLoadCombination` – Returns the Load Combination with the given unique identifier. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_CreateAnalyticalLoadCombination`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_CreateAnalyticalLoadGroup` – Returns the Load Group with the given unique identifier. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_CreateAnalyticalLoadGroup`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Analytical_CreateElementFromCurveMember` – Returns the Structural Analytical Model Surface Member segmented version associated with the selected Surface Element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_CreateElementFromCurveMember`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Analytical_CreateElementFromSegmentedSurfaceMember` – Creates an element from the Structural Analytical Model Surface Member. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_CreateElementFromSegmentedSurfaceMember`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Analytical_CreateElementFromSurfaceMember` – Creates an element from the Structural Analytical Model Curve Member. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_CreateElementFromSurfaceMember`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_DeleteAnalyticalLoadCase` – Creates an Analytical Load Group. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_DeleteAnalyticalLoadCase`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_DeleteAnalyticalLoadCombination` – Adds a Load Case (with its adherent coefficients) to a Load Combination identified with guid. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_DeleteAnalyticalLoadCombination`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_DeleteAnalyticalLoadGroup` – Creates an Analytical Load Group. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_DeleteAnalyticalLoadGroup`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAllAnalyticalLoadCases` – Deletes an Analytical Load Group. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAllAnalyticalLoadCases`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalLinkGeometry` – Obtains the API_AnalyticalLinkType from <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalLinkGeometry`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalLoadCase` – Returns an array of unique identifiers of the existing Load Cases from a Load Group. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalLoadCase`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalLoadCases` – Returns an array of unique identifiers of all the existing Load Cases from every Load Group. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalLoadCases`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalLoadCombination` – Returns an array of unique identifiers of the existing Load Combinations. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalLoadCombination`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalLoadCombinations` – Deletes an Analytical Load Group. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalLoadCombinations`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalLoadGroup` – Returns an array of unique identifiers of the existing Load Groups. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalLoadGroup`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalLoadGroups` – Get Structural Analytical Model's Custom Surface Coordinates for Surface Load from the given memo. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalLoadGroups`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalMemberConnections` – Get the local coordinate system of a member in intersection of AnalyticalMemberPartRefs <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalMemberConnections`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalMemberPartRefCoord` – Returns the guids of elements which are connected to a Structural Analytical Member, given by it's guid. Includes elements which are connected by Structural Analytical Links. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalMemberPartRefCoord`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalMemberPartRefCurve` – Returns the coord of the given Structural Analytical Model's Analytical Member part if it identifies an edge. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalMemberPartRefCurve`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalModel` – Returns the Model Variation used for creating the specified Structural Analytical Model. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalModel`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalModelVariation` – Triggers an update of the Structural Analytical Model specified. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalModelVariation`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalPointLoadGeometry` – Obtains the API_AnalyticalPointLoadType from element , if it is a valid API_AnalyticalPointLoadType . <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalPointLoadGeometry`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalRelease` – Sets the Visibility Settings of the analytical model. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalRelease`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetAnalyticalSupportGeometry` – Obtains the API_AnalyticalSupportType from element , if it is a valid API_AnalyticalSupportType . <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetAnalyticalSupportGeometry`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetComplementaryProjectMemberType` – Returns the curve of the given Structural Analytical Model's Analytical Member part if it identifies a vertex. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetComplementaryProjectMemberType`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetCurrentAnalyticalModel` – Returns the Structural Analytical Model created for the current view. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetCurrentAnalyticalModel`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetCurveElements` – Sets the Structural Analytical Model Member Release to the selected Curve Element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetCurveElements`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetCurveMember` – Returns the elements that have Structural Analytical Model Curve Member associated. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetCurveMember`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetCustomCurveMemberStretchValues` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Returns the Structural Analytical Model Curve Member associated with the selected Curve Element.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetEdgeLoadFromElement` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Sets element.type to API_AnalyticalEdgeLoadID , and element.externalElemData to edgeLoad . <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetEdgeLoadFromElement`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetEdgeLoadGeometry` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Obtains the API_AnalyticalEdgeLoadType from element , if it is a valid API_AnalyticalEdgeLoadType . <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetEdgeLoadGeometry`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetLCSOfAnalyticalMember` – Creates an element from the Structural Analytical Model Surface Member segments. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetLCSOfAnalyticalMember`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetLCSOfAnalyticalMemberInIntersection` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Get the local coordinate system of a member from AnalyticalMemberPartRef <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetLCSOfAnalyticalMemberInIntersection`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetLinkFromElement` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Sets element.type to API_AnalyticalLinkID , and element.externalElemData to link . <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetLinkFromElement`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetModelCheckTypeId` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Deletes an Analytical Load Combination.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetPointLoadFromElement` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Sets element.type to API_AnalyticalPointLoadID , and element.externalElemData to pointLoad . <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetPointLoadFromElement`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetSegmentedSurfaceMember` – Returns the Structural Analytical Model Surface Member associated with the selected Surface Element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetSegmentedSurfaceMember`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetSupportFromElement` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Sets element.type to API_AnalyticalSupportID , and element.externalElemData to support . <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetSupportFromElement`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetSurfaceElements` – Provides possibility to stretch/cutback the Structural Analytical Model Curve Member of a Curve Element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetSurfaceElements`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetSurfaceLoadFromElement` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Sets element.type to API_AnalyticalSurfaceLoadID , and element.externalElemData to surfaceLoad . <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetSurfaceLoadFromElement`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetSurfaceLoadGeometry` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Obtains the API_AnalyticalSurfaceLoadType from element , if it is a valid API_AnalyticalSurfaceLoadType . <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetSurfaceLoadGeometry`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetSurfaceMember` – Returns the elements that have Structural Analytical Model Surface Member associated. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetSurfaceMember`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_GetVisibilitySettings` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Sets the current analytical model's generation settings to no rules. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_GetVisibilitySettings`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetAnalyticalRelease` – Returns the Structural Analytical Model Member Relase associated with the selected Curve Element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetAnalyticalRelease`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetCustomCurveMember` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Returns the stretch/cutback values of the Structural Analytical Model Curve Member of a Curve Element.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetEdgeLoadCustomDataFromMemo` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Set Structural Analytical Model's Analytical Edge Load %Geometry in the given memo. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetEdgeLoadCustomDataFromMemo`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetElementFromEdgeLoad` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the Structural Analytical Point Load %Geometry. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetElementFromEdgeLoad`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetElementFromLink` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the Analytical Member Type of a certain element from the Complementary Project. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetElementFromLink`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetElementFromPointLoad` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the Structural AnalyticalSupport %Geometry. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetElementFromPointLoad`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetElementFromSupport` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Converts an Analytical Link that is associative to a Curve Member or an edge of a Surface Member (known as a "shortest" Analytical Link) to an Analytical Link that is connected to a fixed coordinate. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetElementFromSupport`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetElementFromSurfaceLoad` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Get Structural Analytical Model's Analytical Edge Load %Geometry from the given memo. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetElementFromSurfaceLoad`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetGenerationSettingsToNoRule` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the Structural Analytical Model created for the specified Model Variation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetGenerationSettingsToNoRule`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetMemoFromEdgeLoadCustomData` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the Structural Analytical Model's Analytical Edge Load %Geometry. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetMemoFromEdgeLoadCustomData`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetMemoFromSurfaceLoadCustomData` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the Structural Analytical Model's Custom Surface Coordinates for Surface Load. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetMemoFromSurfaceLoadCustomData`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetSurfaceLoadCustomDataFromMemo` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Set Structural Analytical Model's Custom Surface Coordinates for Surface Load in the given memo. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetSurfaceLoadCustomDataFromMemo`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_SetVisibilitySettings` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the Visibility Settings of the analytical model. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_SetVisibilitySettings`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Analytical_UpdateAnalyticalModel` – Returns the Structural Analytical Model created for the current view. <sub>(was `ACAPI_AnalyticalModel_UpdateAnalyticalModel`)</sub>

## Attribute

- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_AddClassificationItem` – Classifies an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Create`
- ➖ `ACAPI_Attribute_CreateAttributeFolderPicker` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Enables the attribute folder picker functionality for a push check. If the user clicks the push check, the picker will open. Attributes folder pickers can be used to choose an attribute folder. *(user interface)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_CreateExt`
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_CreateFolder` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Creates attribute folders. To create a folder, its full path has to be provided. The command will create all folders along the path, if they do not exist.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Delete` – Deletes an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_DeleteFolder` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Deletes an attribute folder and all the folders and attributes it contains.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_DeleteMore` – Deletes more attributes of the same type.
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Attribute_EnumerateAttributesByType` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Iterates the instances of a given attribute type in the database.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Get` – Retrieves an attribute from the database. — [#1395](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1395 "CreateObjects, CreateLamps: start from the library part's own defaults; take parameters and layerIndex")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetAttributesByType` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the instances of a given attribute type in the database.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetAttributesFromProject` – Loads the given attributes from a project file. — [#1615](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1615 "Import attributes from another project file (append / overwrite like the Attribute Manager) and export them to a file")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetClassificationInSystem` – Retrieves a classification of an attribute in a given classification system.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetClassificationItems` – Retrieves all of the classifications of an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetCreationInfo` <sub>AC30</sub> – Retrieves creation metadata for an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetCurrPenSet` – Get current pen set index. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetCurrPenSetID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetCurrentAttributeSetReader` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns an attribute reader for the currently used attribute set. *(a C++ attribute reader the add-on passes to its own 3D model export (EXPGetModel); what it reads (surfaces, pens, fills, line types, building materials) the attribute commands already return, and its texture data holds only the image name)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetDef` – Retrieves extended information for an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetDefExt` – Retrieves extended information for an attribute.
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Attribute_GetFirstValidAttribute` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the first valid attribute index.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetFolder` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Retrieves an existing attribute folder by its path or guid.
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Attribute_GetFolderContent` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns all folders and attributes of an attribute type.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetNum` – Returns the number of instances of a given type of attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPen` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns a pen by index from the active pen table.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPenNum` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Get the number of pens in the active pen table.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPropertyDefinitions` – Retrieves all of the built-in property definitions that are available for an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPropertyValue` – Retrieves the property value of the property definition.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPropertyValues` – Retrieves the property values of the property definitions.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetPropertyValuesByGuid` – Retrieves the property values of the property definitions.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_IsClassificationItemVisible` – Tells whether a classification item is visible for an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_IsPropertyDefinitionAvailable` – Tells whether a property definition is available for an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_IsPropertyDefinitionValueEditable` – Tells whether a property definition is editable for an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_IsPropertyDefinitionVisible` – Tells whether a property definition is visible for an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Modify` – Modifies an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_ModifyExt` – Modifies an attribute.
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Attribute_ModifyPen` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Modify a pen by index in the active pen table.
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Attribute_ModifyPropertyValue` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Sets the value of a property for multiple attributes. If the property is not available for at least one of the given attribute then returns with an error without changing any property value.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Move` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Moves attributes and attribute folders.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_RemoveClassificationItem` – Removes a classification from an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_RenameFolder` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Deletes an attribute folder and all the folders and attributes it contains.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_Search` – Searches for an attribute by name. — [#1828](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1828 "fontName: fall back to Archicad's font search as GetFontsByName does"), [#1824](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1824 "fontName in CreateTexts, MeasureTexts and the other text commands: find fonts the way GetFontsByName does")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_SetProperties` – Sets the specified built-in properties for the given values on an attribute.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_SetProperty` – Sets the property value of the property definition.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_AddProperty` <sub>AC27</sub> – Makes a property definition available for all of given attributes (it is created if it does not exist).
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_DeleteProperty` <sub>AC27</sub> – Makes a property definition unavailable for all of given attributes.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_GetUserData` <sub>AC25–26</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Attribute_SetUserData` <sub>AC25–26</sub>

## View

- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Change3DCuttingPlanes` – Changes the 3D cutting planes. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Change3DCuttingPlanesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Change3DImageSets` – Changes the 3D image item settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Change3DImageSetsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Change3DProjectionSets` – Changes the parameters of the 3D projection. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Change3DProjectionSetsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Change3DStyle` – Modifies the parameters of an existing 3D style <sub>(was `APIEnv_Change3DStyleID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Change3DWindowSets` – Changes the 3D window settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Change3DWindowSetsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_ChangeAutoIntersect` – Modifies the automatic wall intersection flag. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeAutoIntersectID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_ChangeDocumentFrom3DDefaults` – Changes the 3D document setting default values. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeDocumentFrom3DDefaultsID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_View_ChangeDocumentFrom3DSettings` – Changes the 3D document settings of the specified database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeDocumentFrom3DSettingsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_ChangeGhostRecord` – Change the settings of a Virtual Trace (ghost) record. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeGhostRecordID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_ChangeShowHideState` – Changes the visibility of application. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeShowHideStateID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_CoordToPoint` – Converts a model coordinate to screen coordinate in the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_CoordToPointID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Create3DStyle` – Creates a new 3D style. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Create3DStyleID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_CreateGhostRecord` – Create a new Trace (ghost) database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CreateGhostRecordID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_DeleteGhostRecord` – Deletes a Virtual Trace (ghost) record. <sub>(was `APIEnv_DeleteGhostRecordID`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Get3DCuttingPlanes` – Retrieves the 3D cutting plane settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DCuttingPlanesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Get3DImageSets` – Returns the 3D image item settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DImageSetsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Get3DProjectionSets` – Returns information on the 3D projection settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DProjectionSetsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Get3DStyle` – Retrieves all parameters of a 3D style <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DStyleID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_View_Get3DStyleList` – Retrieves the names of all styles in the project and/or the name of the current style. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DStyleListID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Get3DWindowSets` – Retrieves the 3D Window settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_Get3DWindowSetsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_GetCurrentImage` <sub>AC30</sub> – Gets an image of the current view (3D, or rendering).
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_GetDocumentFrom3DDefaults` – Returns the 3D document setting default values. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetDocumentFrom3DDefaultsID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_View_GetDocumentFrom3DSettings` – Returns the 3D document settings of the specified database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetDocumentFrom3DSettingsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_GetGhostRecord` – Retrieve a Trace record from the database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetGhostRecordID`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_GetGhostStorySettings` – Retrieves the current ghost story settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetGhostStorySettingsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_GetShowHideState` – Returns the visibility of application. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetShowHideStateID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_GetZoom` – Returns the actual zoom parameters of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetZoomID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_GoToView` – Switches to the given navigator view. <sub>(was `APIDo_GoToViewID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_IsAutoGroupOn` – Returns the current state of the autogroup mode. <sub>(was `APIEnv_IsAutoGroupOnID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_IsAutoIntersectOn` – Returns the state of the automatic wall intersection flag. <sub>(was `APIEnv_IsAutoIntersectOnID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_IsSuspendGroupOn` – Returns the current state of the Suspend Groups mode. <sub>(was `APIEnv_IsSuspendGroupOnID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_ModelToScreen` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Projects a model coordinate to screen in the current view.
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_PointToCoord` – Converts a screen coordinate into a model coordinate in the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_PointToCoordID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Rebuild` – Rebuilds the content of the current window. <sub>(was `APIDo_RebuildID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Redraw` – Redraws the content of the current window. <sub>(was `APIDo_RedrawID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_ResetZoom` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Restores the zoom parameters of the current database by a given number of steps.
- 🔀 `ACAPI_View_SetCurrent3DStyle` – Sets an existing style as active. <sub>(was `APIEnv_SetCurrent3DStyleID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_SetZoom` – Sets the zoom box of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_SetZoomID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_View_ShowAllIn3D` – Activates the 3D window and shows the whole model. <sub>(was `APIDo_ShowAllIn3DID`, `APIIo_ShowAllIn3DID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_View_ShowSelectionIn3D` – Activates the 3D window and shows the selected elements. <sub>(was `APIDo_ShowSelectionIn3DID`, `APIIo_ShowSelectionIn3DID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_View_StoreViewSettings` – Stores or restores the current view settings. <sub>(was `APIDb_StoreViewSettingsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_Zoom` – Performs a zoom operation. <sub>(was `APIDo_ZoomID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_ZoomToElements` – Zoom to the given elements. <sub>(was `APIDo_ZoomToElementsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_View_ZoomToSelected` – Zooming to the actual selection. (Even in 3D.) <sub>(was `APIDo_ZoomToSelectedID`)</sub>

## LibraryPart

- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_AddSection` – Adds a new section to the Library Part under creation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_AddSection`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_ChangeAParameter` – Changes a value in an opened Library Part parameter list. <sub>(was `APIAny_ChangeAParameterID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_CheckLibPartSubtypeOf` – Checks whether a library part unique ID refers to a subtype of another. <sub>(was `APIAny_CheckLibPartSubtypeOfID`)</sub> — [#1273](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1273 "Add GetLibraryPartDetails and IsLibraryPartSubtypeOf (library part details and subtype check)"), [#1251](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1251 "Expose ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetDetails / CheckLibPartSubtypeOf (library part details and subtype check)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_CheckLibPartSubtypeOfbyMain` – Checks whether a library part unique ID refers to a subtype of another. <sub>(was `APIAny_CheckLibPartSubtypeOfbyMainID`)</sub> — [#1273](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1273 "Add GetLibraryPartDetails and IsLibraryPartSubtypeOf (library part details and subtype check)"), [#1251](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1251 "Expose ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetDetails / CheckLibPartSubtypeOf (library part details and subtype check)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_CloseParameters` – Closes the Library Part parameter list opened for editing. <sub>(was `APIAny_CloseParametersID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_CompareLibPartUnIds` – Compares two library part unique IDs. <sub>(was `APIAny_CompareLibPartUnIdsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_Create` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Starts to create a new Library Part from scratch. — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_EndSection` – Closes the last opened section of the Library Part under creation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_EndSection`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_Get` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the data for a registered library part. — [#1273](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1273 "Add GetLibraryPartDetails and IsLibraryPartSubtypeOf (library part details and subtype check)"), [#1244](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1244 "Add GetLibrariesOfLibParts command")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetActParameters` – Returns the actual (edited) values of a Library Part parameter list opened to edit. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetActParametersID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetBuiltInLibpartUnId` – Retrieves the GUID of a built-in library part. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetBuiltInLibpartUnIdID`)</sub> *(returns the id of a library part compiled into the calling add-on as a resource; Tapir has none)*
- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetDetails` – Returns the detailed parameters of a registered Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetDetails`)</sub> — [#1273](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1273 "Add GetLibraryPartDetails and IsLibraryPartSubtypeOf (library part details and subtype check)"), [#1251](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1251 "Expose ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetDetails / CheckLibPartSubtypeOf (library part details and subtype check)")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetElemLibPartUnId` – Returns the library part unique identifier of the GDL based element (object/lamp/window/door). <sub>(was `APIAny_GetElemLibPartUnIdID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetHotspots` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Retrieves the hotspots of a registered Library Part.
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetLastValidEmbeddedLPName` – Retrieves the last valid embedded library location. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetLastValidEmbeddedLPNameID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetLibPartLockStatus` – Returns the current lock status of the given library part. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLibPartLockStatusID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetLibPartRefGuid` – This function is used to obtain the reference guid of a library part. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLibPartRefGuidID`)</sub>
- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetLibPartToolVariation` – Returns the library part's creator element type and variation ID. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetLibPartToolVariationID`)</sub> — [#1273](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1273 "Add GetLibraryPartDetails and IsLibraryPartSubtypeOf (library part details and subtype check)"), [#1251](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1251 "Expose ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetDetails / CheckLibPartSubtypeOf (library part details and subtype check)")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetLibPartUnIDStr` – This function is used to obtain the given library part's own unique ID. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLibPartUnIDStrID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetMarkerParent` – Returns the parent ID of the marker, in the parentUnID member of the libPart structure. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Goodies_GetMarkerParent`, `APIAny_GetMarkerParentID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetNum` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the number of registered library parts. — [#1364](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1364 "[3/4] Add GetLibraryPartSections and GetLibraryPartSection"), [#1244](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1244 "Add GetLibrariesOfLibParts command")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetParamValues` – Returns the possible values defined for a Library Part parameter. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetParamValuesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetParams` – Returns the default parameters of a registered Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetParams`)</sub> — [#1703](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1703 "Add CreateSkylights: place skylights in roofs and shells"), [#1395](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1395 "CreateObjects, CreateLamps: start from the library part's own defaults; take parameters and layerIndex")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetSect_2DDrawHdl` – Returns the 2D binary (drawing) section data created from API elements. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetSect_2DDrawHdl`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetSect_ParamDef` – Prepares the parameter section data to save into a Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetSect_ParamDef`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetSection` – Returns an existing section of a registered Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetSection`)</sub> — [#1543](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1543 "[5/6] Add UpdateLibraryPartSections and GetLibraryPart2DPrimitives"), [#1364](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1364 "[3/4] Add GetLibraryPartSections and GetLibraryPartSection")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetSectionList` – Returns the list of sections contained by a registered Library. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_GetSectionList`)</sub> — [#1364](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1364 "[3/4] Add GetLibraryPartSections and GetLibraryPartSection")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_GetUnloadedLibpartName` – Retrieves the name of a Libpart, even if the library is unloaded. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetUnloadedLibpartNameID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_InstallESYMHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback for handling external symbols. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_NewSection` – Opens a new section to write into the Library Part under creation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_NewSection`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_OpenParameters` – Opens a Library Part parameter list for editing. <sub>(was `APIAny_OpenParametersID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_PatternSearch` – Searches for registered Library Parts in the loaded libraries. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_PatternSearch`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_Register` – Registers a Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_Register`)</sub>
- ⛔ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_RegisterAll` – Registers an array of Library Parts. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_RegisterAll`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)") *(ACAPI_LibraryPart_Save already registers the parts Tapir creates, and AddFilesToEmbeddedLibrary registers its files one by one)*

- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryPart_Search` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Searches for a registered Library Part in the loaded libraries. — [#1703](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1703 "Add CreateSkylights: place skylights in roofs and shells"), [#1543](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1543 "[5/6] Add UpdateLibraryPartSections and GetLibraryPart2DPrimitives"), [#1273](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1273 "Add GetLibraryPartDetails and IsLibraryPartSubtypeOf (library part details and subtype check)"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_SetDetails_ParamDef` – Sets the detail parameters of a Library Part parameter section handle. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_SetDetails_ParamDef`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_SetUpSect_2DDrawHdl` – Sets up an empty 2D binary (drawing) section to fill up from API elements. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_SetUpSect_2DDrawHdl`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_ShapePrims` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Virtually draws the shape of a registered Library Part. — [#1543](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1543 "[5/6] Add UpdateLibraryPartSections and GetLibraryPart2DPrimitives"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- 🚧 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_UpdateSection` – Updates a section of a registered Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_UpdateSection`)</sub> — [#1543](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1543 "[5/6] Add UpdateLibraryPartSections and GetLibraryPart2DPrimitives"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryPart_WriteSection` – Writes data into an opened section of the Library Part under creation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_LibPart_WriteSection`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")

## Navigator

- 🔀 `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeCurrLayerComb` – Activates a layer combination. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeCurrLayerCombID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeLayoutSets` – Changes the settings of the current layout in Archicad. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeLayoutSetsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeNavigatorItem` – Modifies the given Project Navigator item. <sub>(was `APINavigator_ChangeNavigatorItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeNavigatorVPItem` – Changes an existing Navigator viewpoint root, group or leaf node in the Project Map. <sub>(was `APINavigator_ChangeNavigatorVPItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeNavigatorView` – Sets the given Project Navigator item and its view settings. <sub>(was `APINavigator_ChangeNavigatorViewID`)</sub> — [#688](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/688 "SetViewSettings fails at ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeNavigatorView (-2130313113) even when writing a view's current values")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeStructureDisplay` – Changes the partial structure display options for the current database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeStructureDisplayID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ChangeViewOptions` – Changes the actual model view options. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ChangeViewOptionsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_CloneProjectMapItemToViewMap` – Clones a Navigator item from the project map to view map. <sub>(was `APINavigator_CloneProjectMapItemToViewMapID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_CreateLayout` – Creates a new layout. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CreateLayoutID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_CreateNavigatorVPItem` – Creates a new Navigator viewpoint root, group or leaf node in the Project Map. <sub>(was `APINavigator_CreateNavigatorVPItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_CreateSubSet` – Creates the Subset in the Layout Book. <sub>(was `APIDb_CreateSubSetID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_DeleteNavigatorVPItem` – Deletes a Navigator viewpoint root, group or leaf node from the Project Map. <sub>(was `APINavigator_DeleteNavigatorVPItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_DeleteNavigatorView` – Deletes the given navigator view. <sub>(was `APINavigator_DeleteNavigatorViewID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_DimStand_Get` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Get a dimension preference by index or name.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_DimStand_GetNum` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Get the number of dimension preferences.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_DimStand_Search` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Search a dimension preference by name or index.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetCurrLayerComb` – Retrieves the index of the current layer combination. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetCurrLayerCombID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetLayoutBook` – Retrieves the Book Settings. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetLayoutBookID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetLayoutSets` – Returns the settings of a layout. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLayoutSetsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorChildNum` – Retrieves the number of children of a navigator item. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorChildNumID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorChildrenItems` – Retrieves the child items of a navigator item. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorChildrenItemsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorItem` – Retrieves a navigator item. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorParentItem` – Retrieves the parent item of a navigator item. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorParentItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorSet` – Retrieves information about a Project Navigator Map or Publisher Set. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorSetID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorSetNum` – Retrieves the number of Publisher Sets. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorSetNumID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorVPItem` – Gets information about an existing Navigator viewpoint root, group or leaf node in the Project Map. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorVPItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorVPItemChildren` – Gets the list of child viewpoint groups/nodes of a Navigator viewpoint root or group. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorVPItemChildrenID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorVPRootGroups` – Gets the list of Navigator viewpoint roots. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorVPRootGroupsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetNavigatorView` – Retrieves a navigator view. <sub>(was `APINavigator_GetNavigatorViewID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetStructureDisplay` – Changes the partial structure display options for the current database. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetStructureDisplayID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetSubSet` – Retrieves the Subset settings of the given Layout Subset. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetSubSetID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetSubSetDefault` – Retrieves the default Subset settings of the Layout Book. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetSubSetDefaultID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_GetViewOptions` – Returns the actual view display options. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetViewOptionsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ModelViewOptions_Get` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Get an API_ModelViewOptionsType by index.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ModelViewOptions_GetNum` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Get the number of API_ModelViewOptions.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_ModelViewOptions_Search` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Search an API_ModelViewOptions_Head by name or index.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_NewNavigatorView` – Creates a new Navigator View. <sub>(was `APINavigator_NewNavigatorViewID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_Navigator_RegisterCallbackInterface` – Registers an object with virtual functions that are implemented by the AddOn. These are called by Archicad when certain events happen relating to the navigator items that are registered by the AddOn. <sub>(was `APINavigator_RegisterCallbackInterfaceID`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_SearchNavigatorItem` – Searches the navigator items of the requested database. <sub>(was `APINavigator_SearchNavigatorItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_SetMasterLayoutOnLayout` – Sets the layout context of current database for drawing. <sub>(was `APIAny_SetMasterLayoutOnLayoutID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Navigator_SetNavigatorItemPosition` – With this function a navigator item can be moved to another place. It can be used with items on the layout map and on the view maps (in case of teamworking on the public and on the users' own view map as well. <sub>(was `APINavigator_SetNavigatorItemPositionID`)</sub>

## General

- ➖ `ACAPI_ActivatePropertyObjectHandler` – Activates or deactivates an add-on's property handler. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_CallCommand` – Performs an operation without opening an undoable session. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_CallUndoableCommand` – Performs an undoable operation. — [#1815](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1815 "Project preferences: Reference Levels page and Floor Plan Cut Plane Settings"), [#1757](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1757 "ImportFavorites: run the import in an undoable command so Teamwork projects keep it"), [#1543](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1543 "[5/6] Add UpdateLibraryPartSections and GetLibraryPart2DPrimitives"), [#1537](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1537 "[5/5] Favorites through the Favorite manager, and library part parameter values"), [#1531](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1531 "[3/6] SetLibraryPartPictureImages: library part pictures from base64 PNG"), [#1470](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1470 "Report failed creation transactions instead of returning element results"), [#1292](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1292 "Phase 0 of optimizations. In-process Criteria Evaluator and Search Commands (#1253)"), [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#1820](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1820 "Run several commands as one undo step"), [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)") *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeAddParHdl` – Frees the memory occupied by a handle to an array of @ref API_AddParType. — [#1703](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1703 "Add CreateSkylights: place skylights in roofs and shells"), [#1395](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1395 "CreateObjects, CreateLamps: start from the library part's own defaults; take parameters and layerIndex") *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeAttrDefsHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all of the attribute definition handles. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeAttrDefsHdlsExt` – Frees the memory occupied by all of the attribute definition handles. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeBeamRelationHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all allocated beam relation handles. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeBeamSegmentRelationHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all the allocated beam segment relation handles. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeElemMemoHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all of the allocated memo handles. — [#1826](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1826 "CreateMorphs: favoriteName, layerIndex and id per item"), [#1802](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1802 "Experimental ModifyCurtainWallFrames: contour, class and type of single frames (#811)"), [#1748](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1748 "Roofs: read and set the planes of each pivot polygon edge (#569)"), [#1702](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1702 "Add CreateShells: extruded, revolved and ruled shells"), [#1671](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1671 "Drawing anchor placement, offset, and pen table control"), [#1292](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1292 "Phase 0 of optimizations. In-process Criteria Evaluator and Search Commands (#1253)"), [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes"), [#463](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/463 "SetDetailsOfElements: modify any element type with the Modify commands' type-specific parameters") *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeParagraphsHdl` – Frees the memory occupied by a handle to an array of @ref API_ParagraphType. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeRoomRelationHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all allocated room relation handles. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_DisposeWallRelationHdls` – Frees the memory occupied by all allocated wall relation handles. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_FreeCWContourPtr` – Frees the curtain wall contour pointer. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_FreeGDLModelViewOptionsPtr` – Frees the GDL model view options pointer. *(memory management)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetExtensionInstance` – Returns the HINSTANCE of the add-on's DLL. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetMainWindow` – Returns the application's main window where the add-on works. *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetOwnLocation` – Returns the location of the add-on in the file system. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetOwnResModule` – Returns the add-on's resource module identifier for loading resources. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetPreferences` – Returns the stored preferences of the add-on. *(add-on preferences)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_GetReleaseNumber`
- ➖ `ACAPI_GetToken` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the token associated with the AddOn. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_KeepInMemory` *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_RefreshTeamworkReserveInterface` – Refreshes the registered teamwork interface. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_RegisterModelessWindow` – Registers a modeless palette in the API. *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_RegisterTeamworkReserveInterface` – Registers an interface supporting reserve/release in teamwork operations. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_SetDllTermHookProc` – Sets a handler procedure for the case of abnormal termination. *(add-on infrastructure)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_SetPreferences` – Saves custom preferences data into the preferences file. *(add-on preferences)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UnregisterModelessWindow` – Ends the registration of an add-on's modeless palette with the API. *(user interface)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UnregisterTeamworkReserveInterface` – Unregisters a previously registered interface. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_WriteReport` – Writes a report string into the Report Window or into a custom alert window. — [#1849](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1849 "Run palette scripts in a configured uv project (uvProject in config.json)") *(report window of the add-on)*

- ✅ `ACAPI_Database` <sub>AC25–26</sub> — [#1125](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1125 "Add GetModelExtent command (ACAPI_Element_GetExtent3D)"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)"), [#371](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/371 "Lessons learned building a Tapir-based automation toolkit (AC29)")

## AddOnIntegration

- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallClassificationVisibilityHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the classification visibility handler function. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallElemSetSaveOldFormatHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for handling the conversion of the user data, the add-on attached to an element set. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallFileTypeHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for handling file operations. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallFileTypeHandler3D` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for handling file operations. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallModulCommandHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback to handle command calls from other add-ons. *(add-on registration and menus)*

- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallModulDataSaveOldFormatHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for converting module data into an older format. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallNavigatorAddOnViewPointDataConvertNewFormatHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for converting Navigator AddOn viewpoints into the current format. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallNavigatorAddOnViewPointDataMergeHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for handling the Navigator AddOn viewpoint merge operations. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallNavigatorAddOnViewPointDataSaveOldFormatHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs a callback procedure for converting Navigator AddOn viewpoints into an older format. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPanelHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the creation and destruction handlers for the registered custom settings panel. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPublisherDefaultTranslatorGetter` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the default translator getter function for the Publisher. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPublisherFormatInfoGetter` <sub>AC28–30</sub> – Installs the format info getter function for the Publisher. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPublisherSaveInitialOption` <sub>AC30</sub> – Installs the option initializer methods for the Publisher. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPublisherSaveOption` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the option methods for the Publisher. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPublisherSaveTranslator` <sub>AC30</sub> – Installs the translator methods for the Publisher. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPublisherSaveWithOptionMethod` <sub>AC30</sub> – Installs the save methods for the Publisher with the set options. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPublisherSaveWithTranslatorMethod` <sub>AC30</sub> – Installs the save methods for the Publisher with a selected translator. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallPublisherTranslatorGetter` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the translator getter function for the Publisher *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_InstallSettingsHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the create and destroy handlers for the registered settings observer. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_RegisterAttributePanel` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Appends a custom panel to an attribute settings dialog. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_RegisterBuiltInLibrary` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers built-in library parts to be loaded. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_RegisterClassificationVisibilityHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Tells Archicad that your add-on wants to modify classification visibility. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_RegisterFileType` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers a file type for I/O type add-ons. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_RegisterInfoBoxPanel` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Appends a custom panel to the info box. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_RegisterModulDataHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers the add-on to be called for merging and converting its module data. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_RegisterNavigatorAddOnViewPointDataHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers the add-on to be called when Navigator AddOn viewpoints are merged, saved to an older format, or converted from an older format to the current format. *(add-on registration and menus)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_RegisterPublishFormat` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers a publish format which can be used in the publisher tree. *(add-on registration and menus)*
