- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnIntegration_RegisterAttributeImportFormat` <sub>AC27–28</sub> – Registers an attribute manager format which can be used in attribute manager import. *(add-on registration and menus)*

## Database

- ➖ `ACAPI_Database_BuildWindowValidator` – Registers a new window validator for the given guid. <sub>(was `APIDb_BuildWindowValidatorID`)</sub> *(validators of the add-on's own windows (add-on infrastructure))*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_ChangeCurrentDatabase` – Changes the current (active) database. <sub>(was `APIDb_ChangeCurrentDatabaseID`)</sub> — [#1809](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1809 "ReserveElements/ReleaseElements: report GUIDs that are not elements instead of success"), [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes")
- ➖ `ACAPI_Database_CheckWindowValidator` – Cheks the already added list of elements and checksums form the validator (identified by the guid) against the lists from the parameter if there was any changes. <sub>(was `APIDb_CheckWindowValidatorID`)</sub> *(validators of the add-on's own windows (add-on infrastructure))*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_DeleteDatabase` – Deletes an independent detail, worksheet, layout, or master layout database. <sub>(was `APIDb_DeleteDatabaseID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_Database_DoClip` – Clips the elements created during a clipping session. <sub>(was `APIDb_DoClipID`)</sub> *(clipping session for the add-on's own drawing primitive calls, no effect a JSON caller can see)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetContainingDatabase` – Obtains the unique ID of database contains the given element. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetContainingDatabaseID`)</sub> — [#1809](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1809 "ReserveElements/ReleaseElements: report GUIDs that are not elements instead of success")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetCurrentDatabase` – Returns the identifier of the current (active) database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetCurrentDatabaseID`)</sub> — [#1809](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1809 "ReserveElements/ReleaseElements: report GUIDs that are not elements instead of success")
- ➖ `ACAPI_Database_GetDBUnIdFromGuid` – Returns the old database identifiers from the database GUID. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetDBUnIdFromGuidID`)</sub> *(converts to the old userId/databaseId pair, which no JSON command or official command takes)*
- ⛔ `ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForType` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Helper function to collect all Archicad databases of the given type. — [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)") *(returns what the per-type ACAPI_Database_Get*Databases calls of GetDatabases already return)*
- ⛔ `ACAPI_Database_GetDatabasesForTypes` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Helper function to collect all Archicad databases of the given types. — [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)") *(returns what the per-type calls of GetDatabases already return, without each database's type, which the response needs)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetDetailDatabases` – Returns the list of unique IDs of all detail databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetDetailDatabasesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetDocumentFrom3DDatabases` – Returns the list of unique IDs of all document 3D databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetDocumentFrom3DDatabasesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetElevationDatabases` – Returns all the elevation databases in the project. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetElevationDatabasesID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_Database_GetGuidFromDBUnId` – Returns the GUID from old database identifiers. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetGuidFromDBUnIdID`)</sub> *(converts from the old userId/databaseId pair, which no JSON command or official command gives)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetInteriorElevationDatabases` – Returns all the interior elevation databases in the project. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetInteriorElevationDatabasesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetLayoutDatabases` – Returns the list of unique IDs of all layout databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetLayoutDatabasesID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Database_GetLocOrigo` – Returns the local origin of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetLocOrigoID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetMasterLayoutDatabases` – Returns the list of unique IDs of all master layout databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetMasterLayoutDatabasesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetSectionDatabases` – Returns all the section databases in the project. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetSectionDatabasesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_GetWorksheetDatabases` – Returns the list of unique IDs of all detail databases. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetWorksheetDatabasesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_JsonQuery` – Helper function to call various functions inside Archicad. <sub>(was `APIDb_JsonQueryID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_ModifyDatabase` – Modifies the parameters of an independent detail, worksheet, layout or master layout database. <sub>(was `APIDb_ModifyDatabaseID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_NewDatabase` – Creates a new independent detail, worksheet, layout or master layout database. <sub>(was `APIDb_NewDatabaseID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_RebuildCurrentDatabase` – Rebuilds the current database, even it's associated window is in the background. <sub>(was `APIDb_RebuildCurrentDatabaseID`)</sub> — [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ➖ `ACAPI_Database_RebuildWindowValidator` – Sets the element and checksum list for an already added validator with given guid. <sub>(was `APIDb_RebuildWindowValidatorID`)</sub> *(validators of the add-on's own windows (add-on infrastructure))*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Database_RedrawCurrentDatabase` – Redraws the current database, even it's associated window is in the background. <sub>(was `APIDb_RedrawCurrentDatabaseID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_Database_StartClippingSession` – Sets up a clipping session for newly created elements to be clipped. <sub>(was `APIDb_StartClippingSessionID`)</sub> *(clipping session for the add-on's own drawing primitive calls, no effect a JSON caller can see)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Database_StopClippingSession` – Stops clipping elements. <sub>(was `APIDb_StopClippingSessionID`)</sub> *(clipping session for the add-on's own drawing primitive calls, no effect a JSON caller can see)*

## Teamwork

- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_FindLockableObjectSet` – Retrieves the unique identifier of a lockable object set with a string identifier. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_FindLockableObjectSet`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetHotlinkCacheManagementOwner` – Provides the current owner of the Hotlink/XRef Management in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_GetHotlinkCacheManagementOwner`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetLockableStatus` – Retrieves the reservation status of a lockable object set. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_GetLockableStatus`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetTWAccessRight` – Returns if the current user has the specified access right. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetTWAccessRightID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetTWOwner` – Retrieves the TeamWork owner of a given database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetTWOwnerID`)</sub> — [#1808](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1808 "ReserveElements reports success for GUIDs that are not elements, and nothing is reserved")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetTeamworkProjectDetails` – Extracts details from the given location about the teamwork project. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_GetTeamworkProjectDetails`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GetUsernameFromId` – Retrieves the username of a user identified by a short id in a Teamwork project. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_GetUsernameFromId`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_GrantElements` – In teamwork mode this function grants the specified elements to the given user. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GrantElements`)</sub>
- ⬜ `ACAPI_Teamwork_HasConnection` – Tells whether the currently opened project is a Teamwork project. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_HasConnection`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Teamwork_HasCreateRight` – Checks whether the current Teamwork user has access right to create a certain type of object. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_HasCreateRight`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Teamwork_HasDeleteModifyRight` – Checks whether the current Teamwork user has access right to delete or modify a certain type of object. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_HasDeleteModifyRight`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Teamwork_IsOnline` – Tells whether the client is online connected to the BIM server. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_IsOnline`)</sub> — [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Teamwork_IsServerLibPart` – Tells whether the given location refers to a BIM Server Library Part. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_IsServerLibPart`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ProjectSharing` – Returns the project sharing data in the case of a TeamWork project. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ProjectSharingID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReceiveChanges` – Receives changes from the BIMcloud server. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReceiveChanges`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReleaseElements` – Releases elements in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReleaseElements`)</sub> — [#1809](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1809 "ReserveElements/ReleaseElements: report GUIDs that are not elements instead of success")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReleaseHotlinkCacheManagement` – Releases the Hotlink/XRef Management in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReleaseHotlinkCacheManagement`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReleaseLockable` – Releases a lockable object set in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReleaseLockable`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_RemoveLibraries` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Remove libraries from the BIMcloud server.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReserveElements` – Reserves elements in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReserveElements`)</sub> — [#1809](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1809 "ReserveElements/ReleaseElements: report GUIDs that are not elements instead of success")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReserveHotlinkCacheManagement` – Reserves the Hotlink/XRef Management in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReserveHotlinkCacheManagement`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_ReserveLockable` – Reserves a lockable object set in Teamwork mode. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_ReserveLockable`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_SendChanges` – Sends changes to the BIMcloud server. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkControl_SendChanges`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_Teamwork_SendReleaseCommentMail` – Sends the release comment mail. <sub>(was `ACAPI_TeamworkReserveInterface_SendReleaseCommentMail`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)") *(needs the ids of a Teamwork reserve interface the add-on registered for its own dialog (add-on infrastructure))*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Teamwork_UploadLibraries` <sub>AC29–30</sub> – Uploads the given libraries to the BIMcloud server.

- ✅ `ACAPI_GraphicalOverride_SetOverrideRuleGroupOrder` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Sets the order of rule groups in the current project.

## Property

- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_ChangePropertyDefinition` – Changes an already existing property definition in the current plan (identified by its guid).
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_ChangePropertyGroup` – Changes an already existing property group in the current plan (identified by its guid).

- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_CreatePropertyDefinition` – Creates a property definition in the current plan.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_CreatePropertyGroup` – Creates a property group in the current plan.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_DeletePropertyDefinition` – Deletes a property definition in the current plan.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_DeletePropertyGroup` – Deletes a property group and all of the property definitions inside in the current plan.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyDefinition` – Retrieves one property definition identified by its guid. — [#1292](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1292 "Phase 0 of optimizations. In-process Criteria Evaluator and Search Commands (#1253)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyDefinitions` – Retrieves all the property definitions from a given group. — [#1292](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1292 "Phase 0 of optimizations. In-process Criteria Evaluator and Search Commands (#1253)")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyExprReferenceString` – Returns the string that can be used in expressions to refer to a specific property. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetPropertyExprReferenceStringID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyGroup` – Retrieves one property group identified by its guid.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyGroups` – Retrieves all the property groups. — [#1292](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1292 "Phase 0 of optimizations. In-process Criteria Evaluator and Search Commands (#1253)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_GetPropertyValueString` – Convert an API property value to display string. — [#1537](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1537 "[5/5] Favorites through the Favorite manager, and library part parameter values")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_Import` – Imports a property xml.
- ➖ `ACAPI_Property_InstallPropertyObjectHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the property handler function for the given menu item. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Property_InstallPropertyVisibilityHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the property visibility handler function. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- 🔀 `ACAPI_Property_IsValidValue` – Returns true if the value is acceptable for the given property definition.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_ModifyPropertyValue` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Sets the value of a property for multiple elements. If the property is not available for at least one of the given element then returns with an error without changing any property value.
- ➖ `ACAPI_Property_RegisterPropertyObjectHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Registers a property handler. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Property_RegisterPropertyVisibilityHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Tells Archicad that your add-on wants to modify property visibility. *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_SetPropertyValueFromString` – Sets the value of a property value from a passed string.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_AddProperty` <sub>AC27</sub> – Makes a property definition available for all of given elements (it is created if it does not exist).
- ✅ `ACAPI_Property_DeleteProperty` <sub>AC27</sub> – Makes a property definition unavailable for all of given elements.

- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_CopyFilesIntoLibrary` – Copies files into a folder inside a loaded library. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CopyFilesIntoLibraryID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_CreateCopyOfGdlUserGlobalsHandle` – Returns a copy of the current GDL globals. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CreateCopyOfGdlUserGlobalsHandleID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_CreateFolderInLibrary` – Creates a new folder inside a loaded library. <sub>(was `APIEnv_CreateFolderInLibraryID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_DeleteEmbeddedLibItem` – Deletes the given embedded library item (folder or library part). <sub>(was `APIEnv_DeleteEmbeddedLibItemID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_DeleteEmbeddedLibItems` – Delete embeded lib items. <sub>(was `APIEnv_DeleteEmbeddedLibItemsID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_DestroyCopyOfGdlUserGlobalsHandle` – Destroys the previously copied GDL globals handle. <sub>(was `APIEnv_DestroyCopyOfGdlUserGlobalsHandleID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_GetGDLDate` – Returns GDL environment date information (GDL_PROJECT_DATE). <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetGDLDateID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_GetLibPartOrigLocation` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the original location of a library part. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLibPartOrigLocationID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_GetLibraries` – Returns the list of loaded libraries. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetLibrariesID`)</sub>
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_InterpretGDLScript3D` – Interprets a 3D GDL script with the same settings as GDL Editor. <sub>(was `APIAny_InterpretGDLScript3DID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_NewLibDial` – Instructs the user to select a new library folder in a dialog. <sub>(was `APIEnv_NewLibDialID`)</sub> *(user interface)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_OpenLibraryPart` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Opens the given library part in the Library Part Editor window. <sub>(was `APIDo_OpenLibraryPartID`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- 🔀 `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_OverwriteLibPart` – Sets the overwrite mode for the library part create function. <sub>(was `APIEnv_OverwriteLibPartID`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_ResetLibraries` – Resets the loaded libraries. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ResetLibrariesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_RunGDLParScript` – Run GDL parameter script. <sub>(was `APIAny_RunGDLParScriptID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_SetLibraries` – Changes the list of loaded libraries. <sub>(was `APIEnv_SetLibrariesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_LibraryManagement_UsableGDLString` – Determines whether the string is a GDL keyword or reserved symbol. <sub>(was `APIAny_UsableGDLStringID`)</sub>

## ProjectOperation

- ➖ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_AdditionalFileWasCreated` – Informs Archicad about the additional file created during exporting or publishing. Required for handling them with the target file. (E.g., uploading to BIMcloud together.) <sub>(was `ACAPI_Inform_AdditionalFileWasCreated`)</sub> *(tells Archicad about files an add-on writes while the project is saved (add-on infrastructure))*
- ➖ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_CatchProjectEvent` – Register or unregister your add-on to be notified of application and project events. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchProjectEvent`)</sub> — [#1834](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1834 "[3/3] Notification clients: attribute replacement events"), [#1831](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1831 "[2/3] Notification clients: window, floor and project events"), [#1813](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1813 "Notification clients: selection, window and project events") *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Close` – Closes the current project window. <sub>(was `APIDo_CloseID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_GetMiscAppInfo` – Gets miscellaneous information about application. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetMiscAppInfoID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_GetMiscPlotterInfo` – Gets miscellaneous information about plotter. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetMiscPlotterInfoID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_GetMiscPrinterInfo` – Gets miscellaneous information about current printer. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetMiscPrinterInfoID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_GetPrinterPars` – Retrieves the current printer settings. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetPrinterParsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_NewProject` – Opens a new plan. <sub>(was `APIDo_NewProjectID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Open` – Opens a new project file. <sub>(was `APIDo_OpenID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Print` – Prints the content of the current window. <sub>(was `APIDo_PrintID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Project` – Returns information on the current project file. <sub>(was `APIEnv_ProjectID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Publish` – Publishes a publisher set. <sub>(was `APIDo_PublishID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Quit` – Terminates the server application. <sub>(was `APIDo_QuitID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_ReloadLibraries` – Resets and loads the active libraries. <sub>(was `APIDo_ReloadLibrariesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_Save` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Saves the content of the current window in different file formats.
- ✅ `ACAPI_ProjectOperation_SaveAsModuleFile` – Saves the selected elements into a module file. <sub>(was `APIDo_SaveAsModuleFileID`)</sub>

## UserInput

- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_CancelUserInput` – Cancels the user input. <sub>(was `APIIo_CancelUserInputID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_ClearElementHighlight` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Removes element highlights from 2D (floor plan and section) and 3D window. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Interface_ClearElementHighlight`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_ClearNeigFilter` – Sets one of the neig types in the neig filter not to be filtered. <sub>(was `APIIo_ClearNeigFilterID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_CompleteUserInput` – Finishes the user input. <sub>(was `APIIo_CompleteUserInputID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_DrawRubberArc` – Draws graphical feedback during an input process. <sub>(was `APIIo_DrawRubberArcID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_DrawRubberLine` – Draws graphical feedback during an input process. <sub>(was `APIIo_DrawRubberLineID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_GetArc` – Performs an arc input. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetArcID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_GetLine` – Performs a line input. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetLineID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_GetMagicWandSets` – Returns information on curve tracing parameters. <sub>(was `APIEnv_GetMagicWandSetsID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_GetPoint` – Performs a point input. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetPointID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_GetPoly` – Performs polygon input. <sub>(was `APIIo_GetPolyID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_InitNeigFilter` – Initializes the neig filter for an input. <sub>(was `APIIo_InitNeigFilterID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_ResetConstraint` – Dissolve the drawing constraint. <sub>(was `APIIo_ResetConstraintID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_SetConstraint` – Set the drawing constraint for graphical user input. <sub>(was `APIIo_SetConstraintID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_SetElementHighlight` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Highlight elements in the 2D (floor plan and section) and 3D window. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Interface_SetElementHighlight`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols") *(interactive input from the user)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_UserInput_SetNeigFilter` – Sets one of the neig types in the neig filter to be filtered. <sub>(was `APIIo_SetNeigFilterID`)</sub> *(interactive input from the user)*

## Hotlink

- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_BreakHotlinkNode` – Breaks the link of the given hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_BreakHotlinkNodeID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_CreateHotlinkNode` – Gets the story info from a hotlink source file. <sub>(was `APIDb_CreateHotlinkNodeID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_DeleteHotlinkNode` – Deletes the given hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_DeleteHotlinkNodeID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetContainingHotlinkGuid` – Retrieves the guid of the hotlink element which contains the given element. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetContainingHotlinkGuidID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotLinkOwner` – Returns the identifier of the hotlink instance the element belongs to (see @ref API_HotlinkType). <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotLinkOwnerID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkInstances` – Returns a list of hotlink instance elements placed from the given hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkInstancesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkNode` – Returns a hotlink node identified by its guid. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkNodeID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkNodeTree` – Retrieves the tree structure under a given hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkNodeTreeID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkNodes` – Returns the list of hotlink nodes. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkNodesID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkProxyElementTable` – Returns the mapping table of the proxy elements and the source elements of the given hotlink instance. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkProxyElementTableID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkRootNodeGuid` – Retrieves the Guid of the Hotlink Module type or XRef type hotlink root node. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkRootNodeGuidID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkSourceStatus` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Returns the status of the given hotlink source file. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkSourceStatusID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_GetHotlinkStoryInfo` <sub>AC28–30</sub> – Creates a hotlink node without updating its cache content. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetHotlinkStoryInfoID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_ModifyHotlinkNode` – Modifies the parameters of a hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_ModifyHotlinkNodeID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Hotlink_UpdateHotlinkCache` – Updates the cache of the given hotlink node. <sub>(was `APIDb_UpdateHotlinkCacheID`)</sub>

- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_ChangeClassificationItem` – Modifies a classification item.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_ChangeClassificationSystem` – Modifies a classification system.

- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_CreateClassificationSystem` – Creates a classification system.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_DeleteClassificationItem` – Deletes a classification item.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_DeleteClassificationSystem` – Deletes a classification system.

- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationItemChildren` – Retrieves the children of a classification item.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationItemParent` – Retrieves the parent of a classification item.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationItemSystem` – Retrieves the system of a classification item.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationSystem` – Retrieves a classification system identified by its guid or by its name and edition version.
- ✅ `ACAPI_Classification_GetClassificationSystemRootItems` – Retrieves the root items of a classification system.

## Window

- ➖ `ACAPI_Window_AddTextWindowContent` – Adds new lines to the content of a text window. <sub>(was `APIDb_AddTextWindowContentID`)</sub> *(the add-on's own windows (user interface))*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Window_ChangeGridSettings` – Changes the grid setting parameters of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_ChangeGridSettingsID`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Window_ChangeWindow` – Changes the current (active) window. <sub>(was `APIDo_ChangeWindowID`)</sub> — [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Window_Check` – Runs the selected checks of the Action Center. <sub>(was `ACAPI_ActionCenter_Check`)</sub>
- ✅ `ACAPI_Window_CloseWindow` – Closes a window. <sub>(was `APIDb_CloseWindowID`)</sub> — [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ➖ `ACAPI_Window_DestroyWindowValidator` – Destroys an already registered validator for the given guid. <sub>(was `APIDb_DestroyWindowValidatorID`)</sub> *(validators of the add-on's own windows (add-on infrastructure))*
- ✅ `ACAPI_Window_GetCurrentWindow` – Returns the identifier of the current (active) window. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetCurrentWindowID`)</sub> — [#1834](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1834 "[3/3] Notification clients: attribute replacement events"), [#1831](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1831 "[2/3] Notification clients: window, floor and project events")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Window_GetDatabaseInfo` – Retrieves information of the given database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetDatabaseInfoID`)</sub> — [#1809](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1809 "ReserveElements/ReleaseElements: report GUIDs that are not elements instead of success"), [#995](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/995 "Section and Elevation commands: CreateElevations, Get/SetSectionsAndElevationsDetails, ChangeWindow fixes")
- ✅ `ACAPI_Window_GetGridSettings` – Returns the grid setting parameters of the current database. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetGridSettingsID`)</sub>
- ➖ `ACAPI_Window_GetOwnWindows` – Returns the ids of the opened windows in an array. <sub>(was `APIDb_GetOwnWindowsID`)</sub> *(the add-on's own windows (user interface))*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Window_NewWindow` – Opens a new window. <sub>(was `APIDb_NewWindowID`)</sub> *(the add-on's own windows (user interface))*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Window_ResetCurrentDatabase` – Deletes the database elements of the current window and sets up a new, empty environment. <sub>(was `APIDb_ResetCurrentDatabaseID`)</sub> *(empties the database of the add-on's own window (user interface))*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Window_ResetCurrentWindow` – Deletes the database elements of the current window and sets up a new, empty environment. <sub>(was `APIDb_ResetCurrentWindowID`)</sub> *(empties the database of the add-on's own window (user interface))*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Window_SetWindowId` – Changes the guid of a previously registered window. Useful when replacing already opened windows by reusing their window object. <sub>(was `APIDb_SetWindowIdID`)</sub> *(the add-on's own windows (user interface))*

## Notification

- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchAttributeReplacement` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Register or unregister your add-on to be notified if some attributes are deleted and replaced by other attributes. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchAttributeReplacement`)</sub> — [#1834](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1834 "[3/3] Notification clients: attribute replacement events"), [#1813](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1813 "Notification clients: selection, window and project events") *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchElementReservationChange` – Sets notification handler for element reservation changes. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchElementReservationChange`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchLockableReservationChange` – Sets notification handler for reservation changes of lockable objects. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchLockableReservationChange`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchSelectionChange` – Turns the monitoring of selection changes on/off. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchSelectionChange`)</sub> — [#1834](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1834 "[3/3] Notification clients: attribute replacement events"), [#1831](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1831 "[2/3] Notification clients: window, floor and project events"), [#1823](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/pull/1823 "[1/3] Notification clients: selection changes"), [#1813](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1813 "Notification clients: selection, window and project events") *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchToolChange` – Turns the monitoring of toolbox item changes on/off. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchToolChange`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_CatchViewEvent` – Register or unregister your add-on to be notified of Project Navigator changes. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_CatchViewEvent`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_ClassificationVisibilityChanged` – Sends out an @c APINotify_ClassificationVisibilityChanged project event notification to the other add-ons to state that at least one classification's visibility has changed. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_ClassificationVisibilityChanged`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_GetObservedElements` – Returns the elements observed by the add-on. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_GetObservedElements`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_GetParentElement` – Returns the parent of the actual element your addon received notification for. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_GetParentElement`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_GetTranParams` – Returns the transformation parameters of the actual edit operation. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_GetTranParams`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_PropertyVisibilityChanged` – Sends out an @c APINotify_PropertyVisibilityChanged project event notification to the other add-ons to state that at least one property's visibility has changed. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_PropertyVisibilityChanged`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_RegisterEventHandler` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Register an event handler in your add-on. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_RegisterEventHandler`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_Notification_UnregisterEventHandler` <sub>AC26–30</sub> – Unregister an event handler from your add-on. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Notify_UnregisterEventHandler`)</sub> *(event handlers, not commands)*

## AddOnAddOnCommunication

- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_AddMDCLParameter` – Appends a parameter to the parameter list. <sub>(was `APIAny_AddMDCLParameterID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_Call` – Call a command of an other add-on. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Command_Call`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_CallFromEventLoop` – Calls a command of an add-on from Archicad's main event loop. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Command_CallFromEventLoop`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_ChangeMDCLParameter` – Modifies a parameter on the parameter list. <sub>(was `APIAny_ChangeMDCLParameterID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_ExternalCall` – Call a command of an other add-on in another Archicad instance. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Command_ExternalCall`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_FreeMDCLParameterList` – Frees the parameter list handle. <sub>(was `APIAny_FreeMDCLParameterListID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_GetMDCLParameter` – Retrieves a parameter from the parameter list. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetMDCLParameterID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_GetMDCLParameterNum` – Retrieves the number of the parameters on the parameter list. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetMDCLParameterNumID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_InitMDCLParameterList` – Initializes a parameter list to pass to an other add-on. <sub>(was `APIAny_InitMDCLParameterListID`)</sub> *(communication between add-ons)*
- ➖ `ACAPI_AddOnAddOnCommunication_InstallAddOnCommandHandler` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Installs the Add-On command handler object. *(communication between add-ons)*

## ModelAccess

- ✅ `ACAPI_ModelAccess_DecomposePgon` – Decomposes a polygon of the 3D model into convex polygons. <sub>(was `ACAPI_3D_DecomposePgon`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
- ✅ `ACAPI_ModelAccess_GenerateModelWithSeparateComponents` <sub>AC28–30</sub> – Generates a model with separate components of the given elements, and switches to that model for subsequent operations. The operation will not be executed if the active sight is the sight of 3D window.
- ✅ `ACAPI_ModelAccess_Get3DInfo` – Retrieves information on the 3D data of an element. <sub>(was `ACAPI_Element_Get3DInfo`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols")
- ✅ `ACAPI_ModelAccess_GetBuildingMaterial` <sub>AC28–30</sub> – Returns the attribute index of the material of a specific body of an element.
- ✅ `ACAPI_ModelAccess_GetComponent` – Retrieves a component from the 3D model of Archicad. <sub>(was `ACAPI_3D_GetComponent`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols")
- ✅ `ACAPI_ModelAccess_GetConnectionTable` <sub>AC28–30</sub> – Returns a table of connected elements and the connecting polygons.
- ✅ `ACAPI_ModelAccess_GetNum` <sub>AC27–30</sub> – Returns the number of existing 3D components.
- ✅ `ACAPI_ModelAccess_GetTextureCoord` – Returns the texture coordinates of the given surface point. <sub>(was `APIAny_GetTextureCoordID`)</sub> — [#1580](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1580 "Build what #1538 left out for lack of the DevKit: texture coordinates, convex polygons, IFC classification references, ghost records, library part 2D symbols"), [#1538](https://github.com/ENZYME-APD/tapir-archicad-automation/issues/1538 "Finish the API coverage: the 116 open functions that can be JSON commands (MEP, IFC, Favorites, library part sections, project and window odds and ends)")
